# Run This Town

Journal: `ep1/quests/minor_quest/mq304_succession` · `MainQuest` · hash `3838929451`.

Journal premise: Seems Hands has some work teed up for ya. Apparently of the urgent variety. Sounds like Dogtown's about to start sizzlin' up again. Hurry up and finish that other job you got from him first. Curiosity's killin' me.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

2234 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 380 distinct direct resource identities; 57 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
